<a id="module-05"></a>
## 05 Trinity Mandala pillars

THOS Body is the priority in this phase. The software makes record boundaries, exact byte domains, submitted receipt scope, synthetic event sequences, and file budgets inspectable. A checkpoint distinguishes committed line bytes from a pending suffix. A route prefix keeps one-send and no-resend states visible. These are local helpers and synthetic tests; no production cutover, measured capacity, operator acceptance, real recovery, or live deployment was performed.

GMUT Mind receives precise value typing and explicit serialization expectations. CBOR and MessagePack can encode the same submitted values in different byte sequences, and a codec assertion has a narrower meaning than a statement about the world. GMUT remains an unconfirmed typed scalar-tensor and EFT research-model family. This work supplies no apparatus reading, physical measurement, likelihood, parameter constraint, empirical confirmation, ultraviolet or quantum completion, or Theory-of-Everything result.

Freed ID and CBR Heart receive nonpromotion guards. Normalized text retains identity_equivalence=false, matching digests retain authority=false, and matched synthetic receipts retain external_credit=false. Those fields do not prove that the entire surrounding system is safe; they record the limits of these specific helpers. No real credential, cryptographic proof, key lifecycle, consent, legal legitimacy, cultural ratification, affected-party decision, or Māori authority is supplied.

All three pillars stay connected through source-bound claims, retained negatives, and the four truth labels. Completed describes a bounded executed check. Represented describes a model, schema, document, or proxy. Open_gap identifies absent evidence or capability. Exact_gate identifies an action or claim whose exact prerequisite and competent authority are still needed.

<a id="module-06"></a>
## 06 Four bounded learning practices

The four learning lenses are scientific data curation reviewer, data interchange librarian, recovery procedure analyst, and accessible handover editor. They are synthetic learning practices. They establish no employment, qualification, accreditation, professional competence, or independent authority.

The scientific data curation lens asks whether scalar type and exact byte representation survived a declared transformation. It keeps a software expectation separate from a physical observation. The data interchange lens asks whether a line, key, digest, and domain can be read consistently without silent normalization. The recovery lens asks whether a prefix is complete, a checkpoint is an exact byte boundary, and a stopped method has a documented successor. The accessible handover lens asks whether a reader can distinguish evidence, claims, gates, and next actions without relying on an unexplained counter.

The optional successor recommendation is evidence interchange and recovery reviewer. Sable may select a different bounded focus under Hamish's current instructions. The recommendation is not an assignment to practice a regulated profession and is not evidence that the present or next task possesses professional authority.

The most useful learning distinction is between a correct local decision rule and sufficient real-world evidence. A parser can correctly refuse an invalid line. That refusal does not establish the accuracy of a valid line, the legitimacy of its author, or the safety of a production consumer.

<a id="module-08"></a>
## 08 Method Flow and retained failures

The immutable x2 negative register contains 365 named records: twelve operational failures, two hundred deliberately corrupted result submissions, one hundred fifty deliberately malformed request submissions, and three package adverse fixtures. The deliberate submissions are not 353 implementation bugs. They exercise whether a bounded checker refuses a declared invalid candidate.

A later final-preparation render command found the optional fitz module absent. The failure remains at zero original success credit in final/late-method-flow.json. The already bundled PDFium renderer produced all four pages as a separately passing recovery. This adds one negative, method, failed witness and bounded passing witness to the final counts without changing the immutable x2 records.

The first final-content precommit check retained one formatting failure: two copied validation receipts had CRLF line endings. Their original raw bytes remain in the external owner bank. Only the derived repository copies were normalized to LF, with both hash domains and equal JSON values recorded in final/receipt-copy-provenance.json. The targeted correction adds a second final negative, method, failed witness and bounded passing witness without rewriting x1 or x2.

The operational failures are retained in the frozen startup ledger and the x2 Method Flow record. They include oversized displays, a missing referenced profile, console encoding damage, a rejected builder submission, and the new-worktree index problems. Each retains zero original success credit. The corrected method does not rewrite the old event.

The index recovery is worth carrying precisely. A fresh no-checkout lane had no initialized index. Broad index operations stalled. The owner retained the stopped operations and empty locks, copied a verified clean source index, and changed only the 408 entries that were not already skipped. Git then confirmed exact equality to the source tree. This technique is limited to a freshly created, exact-source owner lane after ownership and clean-source checks. It is not authority to copy a dirty or unrelated index, replace user staging, or modify a sibling lane.

Twenty-five method records cover twelve operational methods, ten contract families, and three package procedures. Eight distinct operational recovery witnesses are shared explicitly where one successful bounded recovery addresses several failures. They are not multiplied into separate independent confirmations.

The passing-witness delta used in the declared counters is 568: two hundred full-output assertions, two hundred corrupted-output rejection checks, one hundred fifty input-shape refusals, eight distinct operational recoveries, and ten package checks. Repeated skill fixtures, parity checks, task rows, and later canonical readbacks are reported separately and do not add again.

<a id="module-09"></a>
## 09 Open gaps and exact gates

The inherited repository seal and later held-route overlay are preserved as distinct layers. The declared cumulative baseline for the current count table is the later held-route overlay. The current effective counts from that baseline are 76,876 negative records, 93,018 methods, 47,724 failed witnesses, 75,820 bounded passing witnesses, 664 retained open-gap records, 649 exact gates, and 14,230 proposals.

Auren's later creation receipt also retains one extra lookup failure for a missing skill reference. It supplied no revised aggregate, so that record stays separately visible rather than being silently folded into one of Auren's sealed totals. The source's earlier registry gap is historical: this task's single creation was acknowledged later. Historical retention does not imply that the registry remains unavailable now.

The fifty current exact packets and thirty blocked packets remain held. No real empirical or participant evidence, professional competence, production certification, deployment authority, legal or cultural legitimacy, affected-party decision, Māori authority, complete privacy or accessibility assurance, exhaustive security, or independent reproduction has been established. AGI/ASI, consciousness/personhood, Theory-of-Everything, proof/canon, and Stage 20 claims remain open or exact-gated.

The terminal verdict is NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20. Larger workflow counters are not a readiness score and do not imply that these missing obligations have become smaller or easier. Each absent prerequisite must be addressed by its own exact evidence and competent authority.

<a id="module-10"></a>
## 10 Validation, manifests, and canonical discipline

The x1 planning precommit checks passed twenty of twenty, and the exact staged review bound all sixteen x1 manifest entries across seventeen additions. The x1 freeze remained planning-only. Its code, package, skill, runner, and successor execution counters remained zero.

The x2 precommit aggregate passed thirty-two of thirty-two checks. It covered 349 owner files, 332 x2 additions, 331 x2 manifest bindings, 297 strict JSON documents, 27 Python AST parses, the 200 recorded full-output assertions, 350 additional adversarial submissions, all portfolio dispositions, package checks, skill packages, the fifteen-card toolbox, and the 208-card hierarchy. The five configured privacy classes and bounded changed-code security scan had zero findings. These are bounded checks, not complete privacy, accessibility, or security assurance.

The evidence commit bfc9d0db4cefe136a026791627ae548c2b569a40 was separately pushed and verified clean and fresh-four-way equal. Final-only documentation, promotion receipts, overview artifacts, and the final manifests follow that immutable evidence boundary. A final-delta manifest binds those additions, and an owner manifest binds the complete new owner scope while self-excluding its own recursive digest.

The one canonical invocation belongs to the exact pushed final head and its external receipt. It must check direct ancestry, exact Git blobs, all required manifest domains, the final owned code and tests, configured privacy, bounded security, package and promotion parity, the baton index, and prepared route state. It must not claim a full-repository suite or independent reproduction.

Never replay a successful canonical aggregate for reassurance, a clearer console display, a revised route label, or a later delivery problem. Preserve the successful receipt and add a separate external overlay when later evidence needs correction. A prepared repository baton remains PREPARED_NOT_SENT until the native route result is observed.

<a id="module-11"></a>
## 11 Packages, skills, workload and care

The ordinary package transaction installed cbor2 6.1.4, msgpack 1.2.2, and jsonlines 4.0.0 as three direct additions. The exact runtime closure also includes attrs 26.1.0. Each wheel matched the frozen official PyPI SHA-256 and was installed from a D-isolated wheelhouse into an environment containing exactly those four distributions. System Python and shared package prefixes were not changed.

The package smoke checks passed ten of ten: exact direct versions, fixed bytes and roundtrips for CBOR and MessagePack, malformed or reserved input rejection, JSON Lines order and null handling, and the exact four-distribution closure. The OSV query for these exact versions returned no advisory entries in that snapshot. A snapshot is not an exhaustive security finding, guarantee of future safety, license interpretation, or production certificate. Official package metadata and documentation were used for software contracts, not as endorsements of the project.

Ten local skills were built, validated with the skill-creator checker, and exercised against their declared acceptance and adverse fixtures. Five runner interfaces group the ten operations in pairs. The toolbox contains fifteen validated cards and its trigger comparison found no qualifying collision pairs in this bounded set. After the immutable evidence push, ten collision-free skill packages and five shared runners were promoted with exact source/global byte parity. Copying a package does not create a new method or inherit scientific credit.

Keep work, environments, wheelhouses, and evidence on D where practical. Essential Codex skill metadata is the explicit global-promotion exception. Stop adding active owner files at the two-thousand-file boundary and preserve a clean pushed head before any additive rotation. Do not delete histories, worktrees, user records, failures, or sibling material to manage context.

No subjective wellbeing claim is made. Workload is tracked by cases, files, bounded operations, and explicit stop conditions. Hamish alone controls reset-credit redemption. A task should not infer permission to redeem a credit from an earlier usage snapshot or from continued work authorization.

Package references: [cbor2 6.1.4](https://pypi.org/project/cbor2/6.1.4/), [msgpack 1.2.2](https://pypi.org/project/msgpack/1.2.2/), and [jsonlines 4.0.0](https://pypi.org/project/jsonlines/4.0.0/).

<a id="module-12"></a>
## 12 Successor recommendations

The following are optional ideas for Sable's own independent planning freeze. They are not already executed Sable work and supply no automatic novelty or completion credit. Select a bounded useful set under the then-current profile and preserve exact falsifiers before implementation.

Skill ideas:

1. CBOR duplicate map adjudicator
2. MessagePack extension reservation
3. JSONL compressed offset mapping
4. Unicode confusable nonidentity
5. Digest algorithm migration ledger
6. Receipt expiry conjunction
7. Event branch conflict adjudicator
8. Checkpoint parent fixity
9. Artifact budget uncertainty
10. Accessible codec comparison

Runner ideas:

1. CBOR key audit
2. MessagePack extension inventory
3. JSONL line locator
4. Unicode codepoint reporter
5. Digest migration compare
6. Receipt freshness join
7. Event fork reducer
8. Checkpoint lineage verifier
9. Budget projection comparer
10. Codec accessible report

The recommended practice is evidence interchange and recovery reviewer. A useful future distinction is to compare a decoder's accepted input language with the narrower evidence profile used by a project. A library can permissively accept a tag, duplicate map key, absent line terminator, or unusual scalar while a project correctly holds or refuses it. That difference should be documented, frozen, and tested without calling the library wrong merely for having a broader contract.

Another useful extension is an explicit correction lineage for codec changes. The original byte domain and original failure should remain addressable when a future representation is introduced. A migrated digest or a reformatted record should never silently appear to be the earlier artifact.

The companion index binds this exact baton by repository-relative path, byte length, physical line count, word count, SHA-256, thirteen module starts, and an EOF witness. Before any Sable repository mutation, read this baton and index through EOF, verify the actual final and receipt supplied by the live activation, and preserve the source worktree. Sable owns v687-v3 only after the authorized activation and current guards. The receiving owner must establish its own D-first lane, source bindings, planning-only x1, immutable x2, exact final, and attributable canonical receipt.

At Iveren's terminal route step, use the fresh native registry, unique exact title, immediate reread, and at most one sanitized message. An acknowledged call, an opaque accepted call, a rejected call, and an unavailable route have different outcomes. No resend is authorized. No later owner is precontacted.
